Complete toolkit for code reviewer with modern tools and best practices.

# Supabase

## Core Principles

**1. Supabase changes frequently — verify against changelog and current docs before implementing.**
Do not rely on training data for Supabase features. Function signatures, config.toml settings, and API conventions change between versions.

First, fetch `https://supabase.com/changelog.md` (a lightweight summary index — not a heavy pull), scan for `breaking-change` tags relevant to your task, and follow the linked page for any that apply. Then look up the relevant topic using the documentation access methods below.

**2. Verify your work.**
After implementing any fix, run a test query to confirm the change works. A fix without verification is incomplete.

**3. Recover from errors, don't loop.**
If an approach fails after 2-3 attempts, stop and reconsider. Try a different method, check documentation, inspect the error more carefully, and review relevant logs when available. Supabase issues are not always solved by retrying the same command, and the answer is not always in the logs, but logs are often worth checking before proceeding.

**4. Exposing tables to the Data API:** Depending on the user's [Data API settings](https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/<ref>/integrations/data_api/settings), newly created tables may not be automatically exposed via the Data (REST) API. If this is the case, `anon` and `authenticated` roles will need to be explicitly granted access.

> Note that this is separate from RLS, which controls which _rows_ are visible once a table is accessible, not whether the table is accessible at all.

When a user reports a SQL-created table is unexpectedly inaccessible, check their Data API settings and whether the roles have been granted access via explicit `GRANT` SQL. When granting public (`anon`/`authenticated`) access, always enable RLS too. See [Exposing a Table to the Data API](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/api/securing-your-api.md) for the full setup workflow.

**5. RLS in exposed schemas.**
Enable RLS on every table in any exposed schema, which includes `public` by default. This is critical in Supabase because tables in exposed schemas can be reachable through the Data API when the `anon`/`authenticated` roles have access (see [Exposing a Table to the Data API](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/api/securing-your-api.md)). For private schemas, prefer RLS as defense in depth. After enabling RLS, create policies that match the actual access model rather than defaulting every table to the same `auth.uid()` pattern.

**6. Security checklist.**
When working on any Supabase task that touches auth, RLS, views, storage, or user data, run through this checklist. These are Supabase-specific security traps that silently create vulnerabilities:

- **Auth and session security**
  - **Never use `user_metadata` claims in JWT-based authorization decisions.** In Supabase, `raw_user_meta_data` is user-editable and can appear in `auth.jwt()`, so it is unsafe for RLS policies or any other authorization logic. Store authorization data in `raw_app_meta_data` / `app_metadata` instead.
  - **Deleting a user does not invalidate existing access tokens.** Sign out or revoke sessions first, keep JWT expiry short for sensitive apps, and for strict guarantees validate `session_id` against `auth.sessions` on sensitive operations.
  - **If you use `app_metadata` or `auth.jwt()` for authorization, remember JWT claims are not always fresh until the user's token is refreshed.**

- **API key and client exposure**
  - **Never expose the `service_role` or secret key in public clients.** Prefer publishable keys for frontend code. Legacy `anon` keys are only for compatibility. In Next.js, any `NEXT_PUBLIC_` env var is sent to the browser.

- **RLS, views, and privileged database code**
  - **Views bypass RLS by default.** In Postgres 15 and above, use `CREATE VIEW ... WITH (security_invoker = true)`. In older versions of Postgres, protect your views by revoking access from the `anon` and `authenticated` roles, or by putting them in an unexposed schema.
  - **UPDATE requires a SELECT policy.** In Postgres RLS, an UPDATE needs to first SELECT the row. Without a SELECT policy, updates silently return 0 rows — no error, just no change.
  - **`auth.role()` is deprecated — use the `TO` clause instead.** Supabase has deprecated `auth.role()` in favour of specifying the target role directly on the policy with `TO authenticated` or `TO anon`. Beyond deprecation, `auth.role() = 'authenticated'` breaks silently when anonymous sign-ins are enabled, because anonymous users carry the `authenticated` Postgres role and pass the check regardless of whether the user is genuinely signed in.
    ```sql
    -- Deprecated (do not use)
    create policy "example" on table_name for select
    using ( auth.role() = 'authenticated' );
    ```
  - **`TO authenticated` alone is authentication without authorization (BOLA / IDOR).** Using `TO authenticated` only checks the role — it does not restrict which rows a user can access. The correct pattern combines `TO authenticated` with an ownership predicate in `USING`:
    ```sql
    create policy "example" on table_name for select
    to authenticated
    using ( (select auth.uid()) = user_id );
    ```
  - **UPDATE policies require both `USING` and `WITH CHECK`.** Without `WITH CHECK`, a user can reassign a row's `user_id` to another user:
    ```sql
    create policy "example" on table_name for update
    to authenticated
    using ( (select auth.uid()) = user_id )
    with check ( (select auth.uid()) = user_id );
    ```
  - **`SECURITY DEFINER` functions bypass RLS.** A `SECURITY DEFINER` function runs with its creator's privileges — typically a role with `bypassrls` (e.g., `postgres`). Never add `SECURITY DEFINER` to resolve a permission error; it silently removes access control without fixing the underlying cause. Prefer `SECURITY INVOKER`.
  - **`SECURITY DEFINER` functions in `public` are callable by all roles.** Postgres grants `EXECUTE` to `PUBLIC` by default for every new function, so any `SECURITY DEFINER` function in `public` is a public API endpoint callable by `anon` and `authenticated` (which inherit from `PUBLIC`) without any additional grant. When `SECURITY DEFINER` is genuinely needed (e.g., bypassing RLS on an internal lookup table), keep the function in a non-exposed schema, always include an `auth.uid()` check in the function body, and run `supabase db advisors` after making changes.

- **Storage access control**
  - **Storage upsert requires INSERT + SELECT + UPDATE.** Granting only INSERT allows new uploads but file replacement (upsert) silently fails. You need all three.

- **Dependency and supply-chain security**
  - **Always pin package versions and commit lockfiles** when installing Supabase packages (`supabase-js`, `@supabase/ssr`, `supabase-py`, etc.). See the [npm security guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/security/npm-security.md) for the full checklist.

For any security concern not covered above, fetch the Supabase product security index: `https://supabase.com/docs/guides/security/product-security.md`

## Supabase CLI

Always discover commands via `--help` — never guess. The CLI structure changes between versions.

```bash
supabase --help                    # All top-level commands
supabase <group> --help            # Subcommands (e.g., supabase db --help)
supabase <group> <command> --help  # Flags for a specific command
```

**Supabase CLI Known gotchas:**

- `supabase db query` requires **CLI v2.79.0+** → use MCP `execute_sql` or `psql` as fallback
- `supabase db advisors` requires **CLI v2.81.3+** → use MCP `get_advisors` as fallback
- In imperative migration projects, create new hand-authored migration files with `supabase migration new <name>` first. Never invent a migration filename or rely on memory for the expected format. Declarative schema projects generate migrations from `supabase/schemas/`; see "Making and Committing Schema Changes" below.

**Version check and upgrade:** Run `supabase --version` to check. For CLI changelogs and version-specific features, consult the [CLI documentation](https://supabase.com/docs/reference/cli/introduction) or [GitHub releases](https://github.com/supabase/cli/releases).

## Supabase MCP Server

For setup instructions, server URL, and configuration, see the [MCP setup guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/getting-started/mcp).

**Troubleshooting connection issues** — follow these steps in order:

1. **Check if the server is reachable:**
   `curl -so /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" https://mcp.supabase.com/mcp`
   A `401` is expected (no token) and means the server is up. Timeout or "connection refused" means it may be down.

2. **Check `.mcp.json` configuration:**
   Verify the project root has a valid `.mcp.json` with the correct server URL. If missing, create one pointing to `https://mcp.supabase.com/mcp`.

3. **Authenticate the MCP server:**
   If the server is reachable and `.mcp.json` is correct but tools aren't visible, the user needs to authenticate. The Supabase MCP server uses OAuth 2.1 — tell the user to trigger the auth flow in their agent, complete it in the browser, and reload the session.

## Supabase Documentation

Before implementing any Supabase feature, find the relevant documentation. Use these methods in priority order:

1. **MCP `search_docs` tool** (preferred — returns relevant snippets directly)
2. **Fetch docs pages as markdown** — any docs page can be fetched by appending `.md` to the URL path.
3. **Web search** for Supabase-specific topics when you don't know which page to look at.

## Making and Committing Schema Changes

First decide which schema workflow the project uses.

### Option A: Declarative schemas

Use this when `supabase/schemas/` exists or `config.toml` sets `schema_paths`. Edit the desired schema state in those files, then generate and review the migration. Do not start by hand-writing a migration. See the [Declarative database schemas guide](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/local-development/declarative-database-schemas).

### Option B: Imperative migrations

Use this when the project does not use declarative schemas.

**To make schema changes, use `execute_sql` (MCP) or `supabase db query` (CLI).** These run SQL directly on the database without creating migration history entries, so you can iterate freely and generate a clean migration when ready.

Do NOT use `apply_migration` to change a local database schema — it writes a migration history entry on every call, which means you can't iterate, and `supabase db diff` / `supabase db pull` will produce empty or conflicting diffs. If you use it, you'll be stuck with whatever SQL you passed on the first try.

**When ready to commit** your changes to a migration file:

1. **Run advisors** → `supabase db advisors` (CLI v2.81.3+) or MCP `get_advisors`. Fix any issues.
2. **Review the Security Checklist above** if your changes involve views, functions, triggers, or storage.
3. **Generate the migration** → `supabase db pull <descriptive-name> --local --yes`
4. **Verify** → `supabase migration list --local`

# TKT-10 inventory — every 'super admin' site (scout + live-DB sweep, 2026-07-29)

## Decisions (LOCKED by owner + lead)
- New enum value: `business owner` (matches 'super admin' spacing style).
- FULL privilege parity with super admin everywhere; extra privileges come later.
- Assignment: ONLY at business creation (owner's Businesses flow) — the first super admin becomes `business owner`. NOT invitable, NOT assignable via user-edit forms.
- Structural rule: max ONE business owner per business (partial unique index).
- Protection: business owner cannot be demoted/deleted/suspended by peers; only the platform owner can. DB trigger blocks authenticated non-owner actors; edge fns enforce for service-role paths (they know the real caller).
- Labels: EN `Business owner`, FR `Propriétaire d'entreprise`.

## LIVE DB (queried on box)
- 44 policies match 'super admin' literal — pattern: `check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text])` (array lives IN the policy qual/with_check). List obtainable live: `SELECT schemaname, tablename, policyname, cmd, roles, qual::text, with_check::text FROM pg_policies WHERE qual::text ILIKE '%super admin%' OR with_check::text ILIKE '%super admin%'`. Includes 3 on storage.objects (demand_media_*) and public.users trio.
- `current_user_is_business_admin()` helper: `role IN ('super admin','admin')` — 6 policies route through it (edit fn once → 6 covered).
- 17 public functions with 'super admin' in prosrc: admin_analytics_stats, approve_sheet_order, chat_customer_orders_count, chat_customer_orders_page, current_user_is_business_admin, close_mediabuyer_month, close_seller_month, mediabuyer_month_orders, pay_mediabuyer, pay_seller, refresh_all_open_seller_tickets, refresh_open_mediabuyer_ticket, refresh_open_seller_ticket, seller_month_orders, seller_roster_stats, sync_imported_orders, update_business_profile_fields.

     7	-- transaction commits — even the adding session can't use it earlier). Every
     8	-- policy/function/index/trigger that references 'business owner' therefore
     9	-- lives in the FOLLOWING migration (20260729150000_business_owner_parity.sql),
    10	-- which runs in its own later transaction after this one has committed.
    11	ALTER TYPE public.user_role ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS 'business owner';
-- TKT-10: 'business owner' role — full DB-side privilege parity with
-- 'super admin', plus structural + runtime protection.
--
-- Depends on 20260729140000_business_owner_enum.sql having already
-- committed the enum value in an earlier transaction (see that file's
-- header for why the split is required).
--
-- Decisions (owner + lead, 2026-07-29): FULL privilege parity with
-- 'super admin' everywhere it appears as a role check; assignment happens
-- only at business-creation time (handled in the owner Businesses flow, not
-- here); max ONE business owner per business; the business owner cannot be
-- demoted/deleted/suspended by a peer — only the platform owner (role
-- 'owner') can, enforced here for every authenticated actor (edge functions
-- enforce their own caller checks on the service-role path).
--
-- Sources: every statement below was generated by splicing 'business owner'
-- into the LIVE catalog definition dumped via db.sh (pg_policies.qual /
-- with_check, pg_get_functiondef) — not retyped by hand and not sourced from
-- older migration files, which can drift from what's actually deployed.

BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 1. Helper: current_user_is_business_admin() — add 'business owner'.
--    6 policies (public.orders x3, whatsapp.quick_replies x2,
--    whatsapp.whatsapp_instances x1) route through this helper rather than
--    inlining a role array, so editing it once covers them without a
--    separate ALTER POLICY per policy.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.current_user_is_business_admin()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.users u
    WHERE u.id = auth.uid() AND u.role IN ('super admin','admin','business owner')
  );
$function$;

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 2. Policies — every one of the 44 live policies whose qual/with_check
--    matched 'super admin' (query: SELECT ... FROM pg_policies WHERE
--    qual::text ILIKE '%super admin%' OR with_check::text ILIKE '%super
--    admin%'), regenerated with 'business owner'::text spliced into each
--    role array/ANY(ARRAY[...]) literal. cmd and roles are unchanged
--    (ALTER POLICY cannot change either, and the TO clause is omitted so
--    the existing roles grant is preserved as-is).
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- public.campaign_source_locks (1 policy)
ALTER POLICY "lock_select" ON public.campaign_source_locks
  USING ((("DigiCom Business" = ( SELECT users."DigiCom Business"
   FROM users
  WHERE (users.id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)))) OR ( SELECT is_owner() AS is_owner) OR ( SELECT check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text, 'business owner'::text]) AS check_user_role)));

-- public.courier_payment_note_orders (1 policy)
ALTER POLICY "courier_payment_note_orders_admin_write" ON public.courier_payment_note_orders
  USING ((("DigiCom Business" = ( SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id() AS current_user_digicom_business_id)) AND ( SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text, 'business owner'::text]) AS check_user_role)))
  WITH CHECK ((("DigiCom Business" = ( SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id() AS current_user_digicom_business_id)) AND ( SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text, 'business owner'::text]) AS check_user_role)));

  USING (((bucket_id = 'business-stamps'::text) AND (name ~ '^profile/[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}/'::text) AND (( SELECT is_owner() AS is_owner) OR (((split_part(name, '/'::text, 2))::uuid = ( SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id() AS current_user_digicom_business_id)) AND check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text, 'business owner'::text])))));

  WITH CHECK (((bucket_id = 'business-stamps'::text) AND (name ~ '^profile/[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}/'::text) AND (( SELECT is_owner() AS is_owner) OR (((split_part(name, '/'::text, 2))::uuid = ( SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id() AS current_user_digicom_business_id)) AND check_user_role(( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid), ARRAY['super admin'::text, 'admin'::text, 'business owner'::text])))));

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- 3. Functions — 16 of the 17 inventoried functions contain an actual role
--    check with 'super admin'; each is CREATE OR REPLACEd from its live
--    pg_get_functiondef() dump with 'business owner' added to that role
--    check only, everything else byte-identical (SECURITY DEFINER /
--    search_path clauses preserved exactly as dumped).
--    The 17th, seller_roster_stats, is NOT included here: its only
--    'super admin' occurrence is a code COMMENT referencing the frontend
--    route's allowedRoles array — the actual authz gate in that function's
--    body is current_user_is_business_admin() (edited in section 1 above),
--    so no direct edit is needed or made.
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- current_user_is_business_admin
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.current_user_is_business_admin()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
  SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM public.users u
    WHERE u.id = auth.uid() AND u.role IN ('super admin','admin','business owner')
  );
$function$;

-- admin_analytics_stats
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_analytics_stats(p_business uuid, p_from date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_to date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_sections text[] DEFAULT NULL::text[])
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE
 SET search_path TO ''
AS $function$
declare
    v_now       timestamptz := now();
    v_today     date := (now() at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date;
    v_start     timestamptz := case when p_from is not null
                                    then p_from::timestamp at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca' end;
    v_end_excl  timestamptz := case when p_to is not null
                                    then (p_to + 1)::timestamp at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca' end;
    v_has_win   boolean := (p_from is not null or p_to is not null);
    v_from_day  date := coalesce(p_from, date '1970-01-01');
    v_to_day    date;  -- assigned below (declare-order limitation safety)
    v_profitability jsonb;
    v_health    jsonb;
    v_orders    jsonb;
    v_ads       jsonb;
    v_shipping  jsonb;
    v_inventory jsonb;
    v_channels  jsonb;
    v_customers jsonb;
    v_sellers   jsonb;
    v_couriers  jsonb;
    v_mediabuyers jsonb;
    v_users     jsonb;
    v_all       boolean := (p_sections is null);
    v_out       jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
begin
    -- ADMIN GATE (D3 review — corrects the design contract's "no scope guard"
    -- lock): this payload aggregates business-wide P&L, ad spend, ROAS and
    -- per-collaborator performance. SECURITY INVOKER + RLS already prevents
    -- privilege escalation (payments tables are own-rows-or-admin scoped), but
    -- campaign_daily_entries/orders carry tenant-wide RLS, so an authenticated
    -- non-admin could otherwise call this RPC directly and receive a
    -- pre-aggregated spend/ROAS/profit payload the MB-facing data rule forbids.
    -- The consumer page is admin-only, so this regresses no legitimate caller.
    if not (select public.current_user_is_business_admin()) then
        raise exception 'admin_analytics_stats: admin only' using errcode = '42501';
    end if;

    v_to_day := coalesce(p_to, v_today);

    if v_all or 'profitability' = any(p_sections) then
    -- ───────────────────── profitability (recon 02 §1 / 03 §1) ─────────────
    -- Pool: replace-excluded. Per-stream window anchors are NOT unified
    -- (contract rule): revenue/COGS/shipping/provisional anchor on
    -- coalesce(date_livraison, updated_at); return fees on updated_at;
    -- expenses cash-basis on expenses.date; seller salaries on sde.date;
    -- MB payouts pro-rated by payout-month overlap; product ads pro-rated by
    -- inclusive-day range overlap; campaign entries on entry_date; the
    -- Conversion denominator alone anchors on placement.
    -- Trend = per-day component arrays (product-ads per day = amount ÷
    -- total_days over range ∩ window; MB salary per day = total ÷
    -- days-in-month over payout month ∩ window — exact day-bucket equivalents
    -- of overlapFraction/payoutMonthOverlap; recon 03 §1 RPC shape).
    with pool as (
        select o.id, o.date, o.price, o.raw_price, o.shipping_cost,
               o.include_delivery_in_total, o.courier_id, o.company_id,
               o.master_city_id, o.updated_at,
               coalesce(o.date_livraison, o.updated_at) as delivery_ts,
               coalesce(ps.is_delivered, false) as is_delivered,
               coalesce(os.is_paid, false)      as is_paid,
               coalesce(os.is_unpaid, false)    as is_unpaid,
               coalesce(ps.is_returned, false)  as is_returned
        from public.orders o
        left join public.order_statuses os on os.id = o.order_status_id
        left join public.package_statuses ps on ps.id = o.package_status_id
        where o."DigiCom Business" = p_business
          and o.date_deleted is null
          and o.source_review_status <> 'pending'
          and o.replace is distinct from true
    ),
    -- delivered rows with delivery anchor in window: the revenue (paid) and
    -- provisional (unpaid) universes. FULL pureRevenue chain + cogsForOrder
    -- (accessory part INDEPENDENT of product qty — no `continue`, unlike the
    -- D2 sellers-fin replication).
    fin_orders as (
        select p.id, p.is_paid, p.is_unpaid, p.shipping_cost, p.delivery_ts,
               (case when coalesce(li.line_sum, 0) > 0 then li.line_sum
                     when coalesce(p.raw_price, 0) > 0 then p.raw_price
                     else case when p.include_delivery_in_total is false
                               then coalesce(p.price, 0)
                               else greatest(0, coalesce(p.price, 0) - coalesce(p.shipping_cost, 0)) end end)
             + (case when p.include_delivery_in_total is true then coalesce(p.shipping_cost, 0) else 0 end)
                 as revenue,
               coalesce(li.cogs, 0) as cogs
        from pool p
        left join lateral (
            select
                sum(case when coalesce(oi.total_price, 0) > 0 then oi.total_price
                         else coalesce(oi.unit_price, 0) * oi.quantity end)
                    filter (where oi.product_id is not null and coalesce(oi.quantity, 0) > 0) as line_sum,
                sum(
                    coalesce(case when oi.product_id is not null and coalesce(oi.quantity, 0) > 0 then
                        (case when coalesce(oi.product_unit_cost_snapshot, 0) > 0
                              then oi.product_unit_cost_snapshot
                              else coalesce(pr.cost_price, 0) end) * oi.quantity end, 0)
                  + coalesce(case when oi.accessory_id is not null and coalesce(oi.accessory_quantity, 0) > 0 then
                        (case when coalesce(oi.accessory_unit_price, 0) > 0
                              then oi.accessory_unit_price
                              else coalesce(ac.cost_price, 0) end) * oi.accessory_quantity end, 0)
                ) as cogs
            from public.order_items oi
            left join public.products pr on pr.id = oi.product_id
            left join public.accessories ac on ac.id = oi.accessory_id
            where oi.order_id = p.id
        ) li on true
        where p.is_delivered
          and p.delivery_ts is not null
          and (v_start is null or p.delivery_ts >= v_start)
          and (case when v_end_excl is not null then p.delivery_ts < v_end_excl else p.delivery_ts <= v_now end)
    ),
    -- returned orders anchored on updated_at; fee lookup = courier PRECEDENCE
    -- (courier_id set → courier_fees hit or 0, company NEVER consulted), else
    -- company_fees, 0 without a city. Uniq (entity, city, biz) index → the
    -- scalar subqueries are single-row.
    ret as (
        select p.updated_at,
               case when p.master_city_id is null then 0
                    when p.courier_id is not null then
                        coalesce((select coalesce(cf.return_fee, 0)
                                  from public.courier_fees cf
                                  where cf."DigiCom Business" = p_business
                                    and cf.courier_id = p.courier_id
                                    and cf.master_city_id = p.master_city_id
                                  limit 1), 0)
                    when p.company_id is not null then
                        coalesce((select coalesce(cof.return_fee, 0)
                                  from public.company_fees cof
                                  where cof."DigiCom Business" = p_business
                                    and cof.company_id = p.company_id
                                    and cof.master_city_id = p.master_city_id
                                  limit 1), 0)
                    else 0 end as return_fee
        from pool p
        where p.is_returned
          and p.updated_at is not null
          and (v_start is null or p.updated_at >= v_start)
          and (case when v_end_excl is not null then p.updated_at < v_end_excl else p.updated_at <= v_now end)
    ),
    placed as (
        select count(*)::int as placed_count,
               (count(*) filter (where is_delivered and is_paid))::int as converted_count
        from pool p
        where p.date is not null
          and (v_start is null or p.date >= v_start)
          and (case when v_end_excl is not null then p.date < v_end_excl else p.date <= v_now end)
    ),
    exp_rows as (  -- strict cash basis: amount > 0, expenses.date (timestamptz) in window
        select e.amount, e.date,
               coalesce(nullif(btrim(et.name), ''), 'Uncategorized') as type_name
        from public.expenses e
        left join public.expense_types et on et.id = e.type_id
        where e."DigiCom Business" = p_business
          and coalesce(e.amount, 0) > 0
          and e.date is not null
          and (v_start is null or e.date >= v_start)
          and (case when v_end_excl is not null then e.date < v_end_excl else e.date <= v_now end)
    ),
    sde_rows as (
        select s.date, coalesce(s.total_earned, 0) as total_earned
        from public.seller_daily_earnings s
        where s."DigiCom Business" = p_business
          and s.date is not null
          and s.date >= v_from_day and s.date <= v_to_day
    ),
    mbp_rows as (  -- payoutMonthOverlap: calendar-month overlap fraction
        select coalesce(mp.total_amount, 0) as total_amount, f.m_start, f.m_end,
               (f.m_end - f.m_start + 1) as days_in_month,
               greatest(f.m_start, v_from_day) as ov_start,
               least(f.m_end, v_to_day) as ov_end
        from public.mediabuyer_payments mp
        cross join lateral (
            select to_date(left(mp.payout_month, 7) || '-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD') as m_start,
                   (to_date(left(mp.payout_month, 7) || '-01', 'YYYY-MM-DD')
                       + interval '1 month' - interval '1 day')::date as m_end
        ) f
        where mp."DigiCom Business" = p_business
          and mp.payout_month is not null
          and left(mp.payout_month, 7) ~ '^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$'
    ),
    ads_rows as (  -- overlapFraction sorts its endpoints; end null → start
        select coalesce(ah.amount, 0) as amount,
               least(ah.start_date, coalesce(ah.end_date, ah.start_date))    as s,
               greatest(ah.start_date, coalesce(ah.end_date, ah.start_date)) as e
        from public.ads_history ah
        where ah."DigiCom Business" = p_business
          and ah.start_date is not null
    ),
    cde_rows as (
        select c.entry_date, coalesce(c.spend, 0) as spend
        from public.campaign_daily_entries c
        where c."DigiCom Business" = p_business
          and c.entry_date >= v_from_day and c.entry_date <= v_to_day
    ),
    sums as (
        select
            (select placed_count from placed)    as placed_count,
            (select converted_count from placed) as converted_count,
            (select count(*)::int from fin_orders where is_paid) as revenue_order_count,
            coalesce((select sum(revenue) from fin_orders where is_paid), 0)::numeric as total_revenue,
            coalesce((select sum(cogs) from fin_orders where is_paid), 0)::numeric as total_cogs,
            coalesce((select sum(coalesce(shipping_cost, 0)) from fin_orders where is_paid), 0)::numeric as total_shipping_cost,
            coalesce((select sum(return_fee) from ret), 0)::numeric as total_return_fees,
            coalesce((select sum(amount) from exp_rows), 0)::numeric as total_expenses,
            coalesce((select sum(total_earned) from sde_rows), 0)::numeric as total_seller_salaries,
            coalesce((select sum(total_amount * least(1, greatest(0,
                          (ov_end - ov_start + 1)::numeric / days_in_month)))
                      from mbp_rows where ov_end >= ov_start), 0)::numeric as total_mediabuyer_salaries,
            coalesce((select sum(amount * least(1, greatest(0,
                          (least(e, v_to_day) - greatest(s, v_from_day) + 1)::numeric / (e - s + 1))))
                      from ads_rows where least(e, v_to_day) >= greatest(s, v_from_day)), 0)::numeric as spend_product_ads,
            coalesce((select sum(spend) from cde_rows), 0)::numeric as spend_global_ads,
            (select count(*)::int from fin_orders where is_unpaid) as provisional_count,
            coalesce((select sum(revenue) from fin_orders where is_unpaid), 0)::numeric as provisional_revenue,
            coalesce((select sum(revenue - cogs - coalesce(shipping_cost, 0))
                      from fin_orders where is_unpaid), 0)::numeric as provisional_profit
    ),
    -- per-day trend components (Casablanca days; client re-buckets)
    t_rev as (
        select (delivery_ts at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date as d,
               sum(revenue)::numeric as revenue,
               sum(cogs)::numeric as cogs,
               sum(coalesce(shipping_cost, 0))::numeric as shipping
        from fin_orders where is_paid group by 1
    ),
    t_prov as (
        select (delivery_ts at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date as d,
               sum(revenue - cogs - coalesce(shipping_cost, 0))::numeric as provisional
        from fin_orders where is_unpaid group by 1
    ),
    t_ret as (
        select (updated_at at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date as d,
               sum(return_fee)::numeric as return_fees
        from ret group by 1
    ),
    t_exp as (
        select (date at time zone 'Africa/Casablanca')::date as d,
               sum(amount)::numeric as expenses
        from exp_rows group by 1
    ),
    t_cde as (
        select entry_date as d, sum(spend)::numeric as global_ads from cde_rows group by 1
    ),
    t_sde as (
        select date as d, sum(total_earned)::numeric as seller_salaries from sde_rows group by 1
    ),
    t_adp as (
        select (a.s + g.i)::date as d,
               sum(a.amount / (a.e - a.s + 1))::numeric as product_ads
        from ads_rows a
        cross join lateral generate_series(greatest(a.s, v_from_day) - a.s,

hook: PreToolUse

  2857	--    away from 'business owner') or deleted by a peer. SECURITY DEFINER so
  2858	--    it can read the acting user's own role row regardless of RLS; search_path
  2859	--    pinned; EXECUTE revoked from PUBLIC/anon/authenticated (callers reach it
  2860	--    only via the trigger, never directly) — box default ACL grants EXECUTE
  2861	--    to everyone by default, so this revoke is not optional.
  2862	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  2863	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role()
  2864	 RETURNS trigger
  2865	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2866	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2867	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2868	AS $function$
  2869	DECLARE
  2870	  v_acting_role text;
  2871	BEGIN
  2872	  IF TG_RELID <> 'public.users'::regclass THEN
  2873	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'protect_business_owner_role: must only be attached to public.users';
  2874	  END IF;
  2875	
  2876	  IF OLD.role = 'business owner'
  2877	     AND (TG_OP = 'DELETE' OR NEW.role IS DISTINCT FROM 'business owner') THEN
  2878	    IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
  2879	      -- No authenticated actor: service-role/system path (cron, edge
  2880	      -- functions using the service key). Edge functions enforce their own
  2881	      -- caller checks before reaching here, so this is not a bypass.
  2882	      RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
  2883	    END IF;
  2884	
  2885	    SELECT role INTO v_acting_role FROM public.users WHERE id = auth.uid();
  2886	    IF v_acting_role = 'owner' THEN
  2887	      RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
  2888	    END IF;
  2889	
  2890	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Le propriétaire de l''entreprise ne peut pas être modifié par un pair.'
  2891	      USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  2892	  END IF;
  2893	
  2894	  RETURN COALESCE(NEW, OLD);
  2895	END;
  2896	$function$;
  2897	
  2898	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
  2899	
  2900	DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_protect_business_owner_role ON public.users;
  2901	CREATE TRIGGER trg_protect_business_owner_role
  2902	  BEFORE UPDATE OF role OR DELETE ON public.users
  2903	  FOR EACH ROW
  2904	  EXECUTE FUNCTION public.protect_business_owner_role();
  2905	
  2906	-- ── whatsapp.profiles view (live-sweep miss, found during authoring) ────────
  2907	-- Two role arrays gate WhatsApp profile visibility/bucketing; without parity a
  2908	-- business owner disappears from the WhatsApp module entirely. Live def dumped
  2909	-- 2026-07-29; ONLY the two arrays changed. security_barrier preserved in WITH.
  2910	CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW whatsapp.profiles WITH (security_barrier = true) AS
  2911	 SELECT id,
  2912	    email,
  2913	    name AS full_name,
  2914	        CASE
  2915	            WHEN role = ANY (ARRAY['super admin'::user_role, 'business owner'::user_role, 'admin'::user_role]) THEN 'admin'::text
  2916	            ELSE 'user'::text
  2917	        END AS role,
  2918	    COALESCE(active, true) AS is_active,
  2919	    avatar_config,
  2920	    created_at,
  2921	    avatar_url
  2922	   FROM users u
  2923	  WHERE "DigiCom Business" = current_user_digicom_business_id() AND (role = ANY (ARRAY['super admin'::user_role, 'business owner'::user_role, 'admin'::user_role, 'commercial'::user_role])) AND (current_user_is_business_admin() OR id = auth.uid());
  2924	
  2925	COMMIT;
  2035	    if v_couriers is not null then v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_object('couriers', v_couriers); end if;
  2036	    if v_mediabuyers is not null then v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_object('mediabuyers', v_mediabuyers); end if;
  2037	    if v_users is not null then v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_object('users', v_users); end if;
  2038	    return v_out;
  2039	end;
  2040	$function$;
  2041	
  2042	-- approve_sheet_order
  2043	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.approve_sheet_order(p_order_id uuid)
  2044	 RETURNS jsonb
  2045	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2046	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2047	 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_catalog'
  2048	AS $function$
  2049	DECLARE
  2050	  v_caller_biz uuid := current_user_digicom_business_id();
  2051	  v_biz uuid; v_review text; v_name text; v_phone text; v_city uuid; v_status uuid; v_new_status uuid;
  2052	BEGIN
  2053	  IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN
  2054	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden: authentication required' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  2055	  END IF;
  2056	
  2057	  SELECT "DigiCom Business", source_review_status, name, phone, master_city_id, order_status_id
  2058	    INTO v_biz, v_review, v_name, v_phone, v_city, v_status
  2059	    FROM public.orders WHERE id = p_order_id;
  2060	  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'order not found'); END IF;
  2061	
  2062	  -- AUTHZ: super-admin of the order's OWN business only. NOT owner (per-business op, /owner/*-confined).
  2063	  IF NOT (public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner'])
  2064	          AND v_caller_biz IS NOT NULL AND v_biz = v_caller_biz) THEN
  2065	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden: admin only' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  2066	  END IF;
  2067	
  2068	  IF v_review IS DISTINCT FROM 'pending' THEN
  2069	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'order is not pending');
  2070	  END IF;
  2071	
  2072	  IF COALESCE(btrim(v_name), '') = '' THEN
  2073	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'name required'); END IF;
  2074	  IF length(regexp_replace(COALESCE(v_phone, ''), '[^0-9]', '', 'g')) < 9 THEN
  2075	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'valid phone required'); END IF;
  2076	  IF v_city IS NULL THEN
  2077	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'city required'); END IF;
  2078	
  2079	  IF v_status IS NOT NULL THEN
  2080	    v_new_status := v_status;
  2081	  ELSE
  2082	    SELECT id INTO v_new_status FROM public.order_statuses WHERE is_new LIMIT 1;
  2083	  END IF;
  2084	  IF v_new_status IS NULL THEN
  2085	    RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'no entry status available'); END IF;
  2086	
  2087	  INSERT INTO public.order_status_history (order_id, status_id, "DigiCom Business", changed_by, comment)
  2088	  VALUES (p_order_id, v_new_status, v_biz, auth.uid(), 'Approved from sheet sync');
  2089	
  2090	  UPDATE public.orders
  2091	  SET source_review_status = 'approved', order_status_id = v_new_status, updated_at = now()
  2092	  WHERE id = p_order_id;
  2093	
  2094	  PERFORM public.sync_imported_orders(v_biz);
  2095	
  2096	  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'order_id', p_order_id, 'seller_id', NULL, 'status_id', v_new_status);
  2097	END;
  2098	$function$;
  2099	
  2100	-- chat_customer_orders_count
  2101	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.chat_customer_orders_count(p_phone text)
  2102	 RETURNS integer
  2103	 LANGUAGE sql
  2104	 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
  2105	 SET search_path TO ''
  2106	AS $function$
  2107	  select case when exists (
  2108	      select 1 from public.users u
  2109	      where u.id = (select auth.uid())
  2110	        and u.role in ('super admin', 'admin', 'commercial', 'business owner')
  2111	    )
  2112	    then (
  2113	      select count(*)::int
  2114	      from public.orders o
  2115	      where o."DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id()
  2116	        and public.normalize_phone_for_customer(o.phone)
  2117	              = public.normalize_phone_for_customer(p_phone)
  2118	        and o.date_deleted is null
  2119	    )
  2120	    else 0
  2121	  end;
  2122	$function$;
  2123	
  2124	-- chat_customer_orders_page
  2125	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.chat_customer_orders_page(p_phone text, p_limit integer DEFAULT 20, p_offset integer DEFAULT 0)
  2126	 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, name text, price numeric, order_date timestamp with time zone, order_status_label text, order_status_color text, order_status_icon text, package_status_label text, package_status_color text, package_status_icon text, product_name text, product_image text, extra_items integer, seller_id uuid)
  2127	 LANGUAGE sql
  2128	 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
  2129	 SET search_path TO ''
  2130	AS $function$
  2131	  select o.id, o.name, o.price, o.date,
  2132	         os.label, os.color, os.icon,
  2133	         ps.label, ps.color, ps.icon,
  2134	         pi.product_name, pi.image_url, coalesce(pi.extra, 0),
  2135	         o.seller_id
  2136	  from public.orders o
  2137	  left join public.order_statuses   os on os.id = o.order_status_id
  2138	  left join public.package_statuses ps on ps.id = o.package_status_id
  2139	  left join lateral (
  2140	    select p.name as product_name, p.image_url,
  2141	           (select count(*)::int - 1
  2142	              from public.order_items oi2
  2143	             where oi2.order_id = o.id and oi2.product_id is not null) as extra
  2144	    from public.order_items oi
  2145	    join public.products p on p.id = oi.product_id
  2146	    where oi.order_id = o.id and oi.product_id is not null
  2147	    order by oi.created_at
  2148	    limit 1
  2149	  ) pi on true
  2150	  where exists (
  2151	      select 1 from public.users u
  2152	      where u.id = (select auth.uid())
  2153	        and u.role in ('super admin', 'admin', 'commercial', 'business owner')
  2154	    )
  2155	    and o."DigiCom Business" = public.current_user_digicom_business_id()
  2156	    and public.normalize_phone_for_customer(o.phone)
  2157	          = public.normalize_phone_for_customer(p_phone)
  2158	    and o.date_deleted is null
  2159	  order by o.date desc nulls last, o.id desc
  2160	  limit least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 50)
  2161	  offset greatest(p_offset, 0);
  2162	$function$;
  2163	
  2164	-- close_mediabuyer_month
  2165	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.close_mediabuyer_month(p_close_month date DEFAULT ((date_trunc('month'::text, now()) - '1 mon'::interval))::date)
  2166	 RETURNS void
  2167	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2168	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2169	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2170	AS $function$
  2171	declare
  2172	  v_close date := date_trunc('month', p_close_month)::date;
  2173	  v_close_txt text := to_char(v_close, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2174	  v_next date := (v_close + interval '1 month')::date;
  2175	  v_next_txt text := to_char(v_next, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2176	  r record; v_earned numeric; v_del int; v_spend numeric; v_camps int; v_carry_in numeric; v_paid numeric;
  2177	  v_total numeric; v_carry_out numeric; v_state text; v_nxt_earned numeric; v_nxt_del int; v_nxt_spend numeric; v_nxt_camps int; v_pt text;
  2178	begin
  2179	  if auth.uid() is not null and not public.is_owner() and not public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), array['super admin','admin','business owner']) then
  2180	    raise exception 'forbidden: admin only'; end if;
  2181	  for r in
  2182	    select distinct mb_id, biz from (
  2183	      select created_by as mb_id, "DigiCom Business" as biz from public.campaign_daily_entries where date_trunc('month', entry_date)::date = v_close
  2184	      union
  2185	      select mediabuyer_id, "DigiCom Business" from public.mediabuyer_payments where payout_month=v_close_txt and period_state='open'
  2186	    ) s where mb_id is not null and biz is not null
  2187	  loop
  2188	    perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(r.mb_id::text || r.biz::text, 0));
  2189	    select e.earned, e.delivered, e.spend, e.campaigns into v_earned, v_del, v_spend, v_camps from public.mb_month_earned(r.mb_id, r.biz, v_close) e;
  2190	    select payment_type into v_pt from public.users where id=r.mb_id;
  2191	    select carried_in, paid_amount, period_state into v_carry_in, v_paid, v_state
  2192	      from public.mediabuyer_payments where mediabuyer_id=r.mb_id and "DigiCom Business"=r.biz and payout_month=v_close_txt;
  2193	    if not found then
  2194	      v_carry_in := coalesce((select carried_out from public.mediabuyer_payments
  2195	          where mediabuyer_id=r.mb_id and "DigiCom Business"=r.biz
  2196	            and period_state='closed' and payout_month < v_close_txt
  2197	          order by payout_month desc limit 1), 0);
  2198	      if v_earned = 0 and v_carry_in = 0 then continue; end if;
  2199	      v_carry_out := v_carry_in + v_earned;
  2200	      insert into public.mediabuyer_payments
  2201	        (mediabuyer_id,"DigiCom Business",payout_month,payment_type,carried_in,month_earned,delivered_orders,total_spend,campaigns_count,commission_earned,salary_earned,total_amount,period_state,earned_frozen,closed_at,carried_out,status)
  2202	      values (r.mb_id,r.biz,v_close_txt,coalesce(v_pt,'commission'),v_carry_in,v_earned,v_del,v_spend,v_camps,
  2203	              case when v_pt='salary' then 0 else v_earned end, case when v_pt='salary' then v_earned else 0 end,
  2204	              v_carry_in + v_earned,'closed',true,now(),v_carry_out,'pending');
  2205	    elsif v_state = 'open' then
  2206	      if v_earned = 0 and coalesce(v_carry_in,0)=0 and coalesce(v_paid,0)=0 then continue; end if;
  2207	      v_total := coalesce(v_carry_in,0) + v_earned;
  2208	      v_carry_out := v_total - coalesce(v_paid,0);
  2209	      update public.mediabuyer_payments
  2210	         set month_earned=v_earned, delivered_orders=v_del, total_spend=v_spend, campaigns_count=v_camps,
  2211	             commission_earned=case when v_pt='salary' then 0 else v_earned end, salary_earned=case when v_pt='salary' then v_earned else 0 end,
  2212	             total_amount=v_total, period_state='closed', earned_frozen=true, closed_at=now(), carried_out=v_carry_out
  2213	       where mediabuyer_id=r.mb_id and "DigiCom Business"=r.biz and payout_month=v_close_txt and period_state='open';
  2214	    else
  2215	      select carried_out into v_carry_out from public.mediabuyer_payments where mediabuyer_id=r.mb_id and "DigiCom Business"=r.biz and payout_month=v_close_txt;
  2216	      v_carry_out := coalesce(v_carry_out,0);
  2217	    end if;
  2218	    select e.earned, e.delivered, e.spend, e.campaigns into v_nxt_earned, v_nxt_del, v_nxt_spend, v_nxt_camps from public.mb_month_earned(r.mb_id, r.biz, v_next) e;
  2219	    insert into public.mediabuyer_payments
  2220	      (mediabuyer_id,"DigiCom Business",payout_month,payment_type,carried_in,month_earned,delivered_orders,total_spend,campaigns_count,commission_earned,salary_earned,total_amount,period_state,earned_frozen,status)
  2221	    values (r.mb_id,r.biz,v_next_txt,coalesce(v_pt,'commission'),v_carry_out,v_nxt_earned,v_nxt_del,v_nxt_spend,v_nxt_camps,
  2222	            case when v_pt='salary' then 0 else v_nxt_earned end, case when v_pt='salary' then v_nxt_earned else 0 end,
  2223	            v_carry_out + v_nxt_earned,'open',false,'pending')
  2224	    on conflict (mediabuyer_id,payout_month,"DigiCom Business") do update
  2225	       set carried_in=excluded.carried_in, month_earned=excluded.month_earned, delivered_orders=excluded.delivered_orders,
  2226	           total_spend=excluded.total_spend, campaigns_count=excluded.campaigns_count, commission_earned=excluded.commission_earned,
  2227	           salary_earned=excluded.salary_earned, total_amount=excluded.carried_in+excluded.month_earned
  2228	     where public.mediabuyer_payments.period_state='open';
  2229	    perform public.refresh_mediabuyer_payment_balance(mp.id) from public.mediabuyer_payments mp
  2230	     where mp.mediabuyer_id=r.mb_id and mp."DigiCom Business"=r.biz and mp.payout_month in (v_close_txt, v_next_txt);
  2231	  end loop;
  2232	end;
  2233	$function$;
  2234	
  2235	-- close_seller_month
  2236	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.close_seller_month(p_close_month date DEFAULT ((date_trunc('month'::text, now()) - '1 mon'::interval))::date)
  2237	 RETURNS void
  2238	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2239	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2240	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2241	AS $function$
  2242	DECLARE
  2243	    v_close date := date_trunc('month', p_close_month)::date;
  2244	    v_close_txt text := to_char(v_close, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2245	    v_next date := (v_close + interval '1 month')::date;
  2235	-- close_seller_month
  2236	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.close_seller_month(p_close_month date DEFAULT ((date_trunc('month'::text, now()) - '1 mon'::interval))::date)
  2237	 RETURNS void
  2238	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2239	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2240	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2241	AS $function$
  2242	DECLARE
  2243	    v_close date := date_trunc('month', p_close_month)::date;
  2244	    v_close_txt text := to_char(v_close, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2245	    v_next date := (v_close + interval '1 month')::date;
  2246	    v_next_txt text := to_char(v_next, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2247	    r record;
  2248	    v_earned numeric;
  2249	    v_units int;
  2250	    v_carry_in numeric;
  2251	    v_paid numeric;
  2252	    v_total numeric;
  2253	    v_carry_out numeric;
  2254	    v_state text;
  2255	    v_nxt_earned numeric;
  2256	    v_nxt_units int;
  2257	BEGIN
  2258	    IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND NOT public.is_owner() AND NOT public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner']) THEN
  2259	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden: admin only';
  2260	    END IF;
  2261	    FOR r IN
  2262	        SELECT DISTINCT seller_id, "DigiCom Business" AS biz FROM (
  2263	            SELECT seller_id, "DigiCom Business" FROM public.seller_daily_earnings WHERE date_trunc('month', date)::date = v_close
  2264	            UNION
  2265	            SELECT seller_id, "DigiCom Business" FROM public.seller_payments WHERE payout_month = v_close_txt AND period_state = 'open'
  2266	        ) s
  2267	        WHERE seller_id IS NOT NULL AND "DigiCom Business" IS NOT NULL
  2268	    LOOP
  2269	        PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(r.seller_id::text || r.biz::text, 0));
  2270	        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(commission_earned),0), COALESCE(SUM(delivered_count),0)
  2271	          INTO v_earned, v_units
  2272	          FROM public.seller_daily_earnings
  2273	         WHERE seller_id=r.seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=r.biz AND date_trunc('month', date)::date = v_close;
  2274	        SELECT carried_in, paid_amount, period_state INTO v_carry_in, v_paid, v_state
  2275	          FROM public.seller_payments
  2276	         WHERE seller_id=r.seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=r.biz AND payout_month=v_close_txt;
  2277	        IF NOT FOUND THEN
  2278	            v_carry_in := COALESCE((SELECT carried_out FROM public.seller_payments
  2279	                WHERE seller_id=r.seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=r.biz
  2280	                  AND period_state='closed' AND payout_month < v_close_txt
  2281	                ORDER BY payout_month DESC LIMIT 1), 0);
  2282	            IF v_earned = 0 AND v_carry_in = 0 THEN CONTINUE; END IF;
  2283	            v_carry_out := v_carry_in + v_earned;
  2284	            INSERT INTO public.seller_payments
  2285	                (seller_id,"DigiCom Business",payout_month,carried_in,month_earned,delivered_orders,total_amount,period_state,earned_frozen,closed_at,carried_out)
  2286	            VALUES (r.seller_id,r.biz,v_close_txt,v_carry_in,v_earned,v_units,v_carry_in + v_earned,'closed',true,now(),v_carry_out);
  2287	        ELSIF v_state = 'open' THEN
  2288	            IF v_earned = 0 AND COALESCE(v_carry_in,0)=0 AND COALESCE(v_paid,0)=0 THEN CONTINUE; END IF;
  2289	            v_total := COALESCE(v_carry_in,0) + v_earned;
  2290	            v_carry_out := v_total - COALESCE(v_paid,0);
  2291	            UPDATE public.seller_payments
  2292	               SET month_earned=v_earned, delivered_orders=v_units, total_amount=v_total, period_state='closed', earned_frozen=true, closed_at=now(), carried_out=v_carry_out
  2293	             WHERE seller_id=r.seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=r.biz AND payout_month=v_close_txt AND period_state='open';
  2294	        ELSE
  2295	            SELECT carried_out INTO v_carry_out FROM public.seller_payments
  2296	             WHERE seller_id=r.seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=r.biz AND payout_month=v_close_txt;
  2297	            v_carry_out := COALESCE(v_carry_out,0);
  2298	        END IF;
  2299	        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(commission_earned),0), COALESCE(SUM(delivered_count),0)
  2300	          INTO v_nxt_earned, v_nxt_units
  2301	          FROM public.seller_daily_earnings
  2302	         WHERE seller_id=r.seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=r.biz AND date_trunc('month', date)::date = v_next;
  2303	        INSERT INTO public.seller_payments
  2304	            (seller_id,"DigiCom Business",payout_month,carried_in,month_earned,delivered_orders,total_amount,period_state,earned_frozen)
  2305	        VALUES (r.seller_id,r.biz,v_next_txt,v_carry_out,v_nxt_earned,v_nxt_units,v_carry_out + v_nxt_earned,'open',false)
  2306	        ON CONFLICT (seller_id,payout_month,"DigiCom Business") DO UPDATE
  2307	           SET carried_in=EXCLUDED.carried_in, month_earned=EXCLUDED.month_earned, delivered_orders=EXCLUDED.delivered_orders, total_amount=EXCLUDED.carried_in + EXCLUDED.month_earned
  2308	         WHERE public.seller_payments.period_state='open';
  2309	        PERFORM public.refresh_seller_payment_balance(sp.id) FROM public.seller_payments sp
  2310	         WHERE sp.seller_id=r.seller_id AND sp."DigiCom Business"=r.biz AND sp.payout_month IN (v_close_txt, v_next_txt);
  2311	    END LOOP;
  2312	END;
  2313	$function$;
  2314	
  2315	-- mediabuyer_month_orders
  2316	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.mediabuyer_month_orders(p_mb uuid, p_business uuid, p_month date)
  2317	 RETURNS TABLE(order_id uuid, client text, phone text, tracking text, bucket_date date, source text, delivered_at timestamp with time zone, units integer, commission numeric)
  2318	 LANGUAGE sql
  2319	 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
  2320	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2321	AS $function$
  2322	  SELECT
  2323	    o.id, o.name, o.phone,
  2324	    COALESCE(
  2325	      (SELECT cp.tracking_code FROM courier_parcels cp WHERE cp.order_id = o.id AND NULLIF(btrim(cp.tracking_code), '') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY cp.created_at DESC LIMIT 1),
  2326	      (SELECT pp.tracking_code FROM company_parcels pp WHERE pp.order_id = o.id AND NULLIF(btrim(pp.tracking_code), '') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY pp.created_at DESC LIMIT 1)
  2327	    ),
  2328	    (o.date AT TIME ZONE 'Africa/Casablanca')::date,
  2329	    lower(o.source_name),
  2330	    COALESCE(o.date_livraison, o.updated_at),
  2331	    COALESCE((SELECT sum(oi.quantity)::int FROM order_items oi WHERE oi.order_id = o.id), 0),
  2332	    COALESCE(u.commission_price, 0)
  2333	  FROM orders o
  2334	    JOIN users u ON u.id = p_mb
  2335	    JOIN package_statuses ps ON ps.id = o.package_status_id
  2336	  WHERE o."DigiCom Business" IS NOT DISTINCT FROM p_business
  2337	    AND ps.is_delivered
  2338	    AND o.date_deleted IS NULL
  2339	    AND o.source_review_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'pending'
  2340	    AND COALESCE(o.swap_only, false) = false
  2341	    AND EXISTS (
  2342	      SELECT 1 FROM campaign_daily_entries e
  2343	      WHERE e.created_by = p_mb AND e."DigiCom Business" = p_business
  2344	        AND date_trunc('month', e.entry_date)::date = date_trunc('month', p_month)::date
  2345	        AND e.entry_date = (o.date AT TIME ZONE 'Africa/Casablanca')::date
  2346	        AND lower(o.source_name) = CASE lower(e.platform)
  2347	            WHEN 'whatsapp ads' THEN 'whatsapp'  WHEN 'messenger ads' THEN 'messenger'
  2348	            WHEN 'instagram ads' THEN 'instagram' WHEN 'facebook ads' THEN 'facebook'
  2349	            WHEN 'google ads' THEN 'google'       WHEN 'tiktok ads' THEN 'tiktok'
  2350	            WHEN 'native ads' THEN 'native'       WHEN 'snapchat ads' THEN 'snapchat'
  2351	            WHEN 'twitter ads' THEN 'twitter'     WHEN 'bing ads' THEN 'bing'
  2352	            WHEN 'pinterest ads' THEN 'pinterest' WHEN 'reddit ads' THEN 'reddit'
  2353	            ELSE '' END
  2354	    )
  2355	    AND (auth.uid() = p_mb
  2356	         OR (public.current_user_digicom_business_id() = p_business
  2357	             AND check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner'])))
  2358	  ORDER BY COALESCE(o.date_livraison, o.updated_at) ASC;
  2359	$function$;
  2360	
  2361	-- pay_mediabuyer
  2362	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.pay_mediabuyer(p_mb uuid, p_business_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_paid_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(), p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
  2363	 RETURNS uuid
  2364	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2365	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2366	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2367	AS $function$
  2368	declare
  2369	  v_month date := date_trunc('month', now())::date;
  2370	  v_month_txt text := to_char(v_month, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2371	  v_ticket uuid; v_txn uuid; v_carry numeric;
  2372	    v_remaining numeric; v_pt text;
  2373	begin
  2374	  if not (public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), array['super admin','admin','business owner'])
  2375	          and public.current_user_digicom_business_id() is not null
  2376	          and public.current_user_digicom_business_id() = p_business_id) then
  2377	    raise exception 'forbidden: admin only'; end if;
  2378	  if p_amount is null or p_amount <= 0 then raise exception 'amount must be > 0'; end if;
  2379	  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_mb::text || p_business_id::text, 0));
  2380	  select id into v_ticket from public.mediabuyer_payments
  2381	   where mediabuyer_id=p_mb and "DigiCom Business"=p_business_id and payout_month=v_month_txt and period_state='open';
  2382	  if v_ticket is null then
  2383	    v_carry := coalesce((select carried_out from public.mediabuyer_payments
  2384	       where mediabuyer_id=p_mb and "DigiCom Business"=p_business_id and period_state='closed'
  2385	       order by payout_month desc limit 1),0);
  2386	    select payment_type into v_pt from public.users where id=p_mb;
  2387	    insert into public.mediabuyer_payments
  2388	      (mediabuyer_id,"DigiCom Business",payout_month,payment_type,carried_in,month_earned,delivered_orders,total_amount,period_state,earned_frozen,status)
  2389	    values (p_mb,p_business_id,v_month_txt,coalesce(v_pt,'commission'),v_carry,0,0,v_carry,'open',false,'pending')
  2390	    returning id into v_ticket;
  2391	  end if;
  2392	  perform public.refresh_open_mediabuyer_ticket(p_mb, p_business_id, v_month);
  2393	
  2394	  -- ── OVERPAY GUARD (owner ruling 2026-07-15: NO advances) ──────────────────
  2395	  -- AFTER refresh, so `remaining` reflects the just-synced live earned.
  2396	  -- Nothing anywhere rejected an overpayment: refresh_*_balance uses `>=` and
  2397	  -- merely LABELS paid_amount > total_amount as 'paid'; the excess then rolls
  2398	  -- into a negative carried_out -> negative next-month carried_in (the -280
  2399	  -- incident shape). This makes "no advances" true in the DB, not by convention.
  2400	  -- Tolerance 0.005 (half a centime): money columns are `numeric` = exact, and
  2401	  -- 0 live tickets carry a remaining beyond 2dp (measured), so the only dust is
  2402	  -- the client computing remaining in float64 then .toFixed(2). 0.005 absorbs
  2403	  -- that rounding and still rejects every real overpay.
  2404	  -- ERRCODE 23514 = a DEFINITIVE server rejection, distinguishable from a
  2405	  -- transport failure of unknown outcome.
  2406	  select total_amount - paid_amount into v_remaining
  2407	    from public.mediabuyer_payments where id = v_ticket;
  2408	  if p_amount > coalesce(v_remaining, 0) + 0.005 then
  2409	    raise exception 'pay_mediabuyer: montant % superieur au reste du % (aucune avance autorisee)',
  2410	      p_amount, coalesce(v_remaining, 0) using errcode = '23514';
  2411	  end if;
  2412	
  2413	  insert into public.mediabuyer_payment_txns (mediabuyer_payment_id, amount, paid_at, note, created_by, "DigiCom Business")
  2414	  values (v_ticket, p_amount, p_paid_at, p_note, auth.uid(), p_business_id)
  2415	  returning id into v_txn;
  2416	  return v_txn;
  2417	end;
  2418	$function$;
  2419	
  2420	-- pay_seller
  2421	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.pay_seller(p_seller_id uuid, p_business_id uuid, p_amount numeric, p_paid_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(), p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)
  2422	 RETURNS uuid
  2423	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2424	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2425	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2426	AS $function$
  2427	DECLARE
  2428	    v_month date := date_trunc('month', now())::date;
  2429	    v_month_txt text := to_char(v_month, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2430	    v_ticket uuid;
  2431	    v_txn uuid;
  2432	    v_carry numeric;
  2433	    v_remaining numeric;
  2434	BEGIN
  2435	    IF NOT (public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner'])
  2436	            AND public.current_user_digicom_business_id() IS NOT NULL
  2437	            AND public.current_user_digicom_business_id() = p_business_id) THEN
  2438	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden: admin only';
  2439	    END IF;
  2440	    IF p_amount IS NULL OR p_amount <= 0 THEN
  2441	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'amount must be > 0';
  2442	    END IF;
  2443	    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_seller_id::text || p_business_id::text, 0));
  2444	    SELECT id INTO v_ticket FROM public.seller_payments
  2445	     WHERE seller_id=p_seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=p_business_id AND payout_month=v_month_txt AND period_state='open';
  2446	    IF v_ticket IS NULL THEN
  2447	        v_carry := COALESCE((SELECT carried_out FROM public.seller_payments
  2448	            WHERE seller_id=p_seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=p_business_id AND period_state='closed'
  2449	            ORDER BY payout_month DESC LIMIT 1),0);
  2450	        INSERT INTO public.seller_payments
  2451	            (seller_id,"DigiCom Business",payout_month,carried_in,month_earned,delivered_orders,total_amount,period_state,earned_frozen)
  2452	        VALUES (p_seller_id,p_business_id,v_month_txt,v_carry,0,0,v_carry,'open',false)
  2453	        RETURNING id INTO v_ticket;
  2454	    END IF;
  2455	    PERFORM public.refresh_open_seller_ticket(p_seller_id, p_business_id, v_month);
  2456	
  2457	  -- ── OVERPAY GUARD (owner ruling 2026-07-15: NO advances) ──────────────────
  2458	  -- AFTER refresh, so `remaining` reflects the just-synced live earned.
  2459	  -- Nothing anywhere rejected an overpayment: refresh_*_balance uses `>=` and
  2460	  -- merely LABELS paid_amount > total_amount as 'paid'; the excess then rolls
  2461	  -- into a negative carried_out -> negative next-month carried_in (the -280
  2462	  -- incident shape). This makes "no advances" true in the DB, not by convention.
  2463	  -- Tolerance 0.005 (half a centime): money columns are `numeric` = exact, and
  2464	  -- 0 live tickets carry a remaining beyond 2dp (measured), so the only dust is
  2465	  -- the client computing remaining in float64 then .toFixed(2). 0.005 absorbs
  2466	  -- that rounding and still rejects every real overpay.
  2467	  -- ERRCODE 23514 = a DEFINITIVE server rejection, distinguishable from a
  2468	  -- transport failure of unknown outcome.
  2469	  select total_amount - paid_amount into v_remaining
  2470	    from public.seller_payments where id = v_ticket;
  2471	  if p_amount > coalesce(v_remaining, 0) + 0.005 then
  2472	    raise exception 'pay_seller: montant % superieur au reste du % (aucune avance autorisee)',
  2473	      p_amount, coalesce(v_remaining, 0) using errcode = '23514';
  2474	  end if;
  2475	
  2476	    INSERT INTO public.seller_payment_txns (seller_payment_id, amount, paid_at, note, created_by, "DigiCom Business")
  2477	    VALUES (v_ticket, p_amount, p_paid_at, p_note, auth.uid(), p_business_id)
  2478	    RETURNING id INTO v_txn;
  2479	    RETURN v_txn;
  2480	END;
  2481	$function$;
  2482	
  2483	-- refresh_all_open_seller_tickets
  2484	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.refresh_all_open_seller_tickets()
  2485	 RETURNS integer
  2486	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2487	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2488	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2489	AS $function$
  2490	declare
  2491	  v_month     date    := date_trunc('month', now())::date;
  2492	  v_month_txt text    := to_char(v_month, 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2493	  v_id        uuid;
  2494	  v_n         integer := 0;
  2495	begin
  2496	  -- Identical gate to refresh_open_seller_ticket. auth.uid() is NULL under cron.
  2497	  if auth.uid() is not null
  2498	     and not public.is_owner()
  2499	     and not public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), array['super admin','admin','business owner']) then
  2500	    raise exception 'forbidden: admin only' using errcode = '42501';
  2501	  end if;
  2502	
  2503	  for v_id in
  2504	    with earned as (
  2505	      -- ONE pass over the view for every seller in every business.
  2506	      select sde.seller_id,
  2507	             sde."DigiCom Business"                     as biz,
  2508	             coalesce(sum(sde.commission_earned), 0)    as earned,
  2509	             coalesce(sum(sde.delivered_count), 0)::int as units
  2510	        from public.seller_daily_earnings sde
  2511	       where date_trunc('month', sde.date)::date = v_month
  2512	       group by 1, 2
  2513	    ),
  2514	    tgt as (
  2515	      -- LEFT JOIN: an open ticket with no earnings rows this month resolves to
  2516	      -- 0, exactly as the per-row COALESCE(SUM(...),0) does.
  2517	      select sp.id,
  2518	             coalesce(e.earned, 0)     as new_earned,
  2519	             coalesce(e.units, 0)::int as new_units
  2520	        from public.seller_payments sp
  2521	        left join earned e
  2522	          on e.seller_id = sp.seller_id
  2523	         and e.biz       = sp."DigiCom Business"
  2524	       where sp.period_state = 'open'
  2525	         and sp.payout_month = v_month_txt
  2526	    ),
  2527	    upd as (
  2528	      update public.seller_payments sp
  2529	         set month_earned     = t.new_earned,
  2530	             delivered_orders = t.new_units,
  2531	             total_amount     = sp.carried_in + t.new_earned
  2532	        from tgt t
  2533	       where sp.id = t.id
  2534	         and (sp.month_earned     is distinct from t.new_earned
  2535	           or sp.delivered_orders is distinct from t.new_units)
  2536	      returning sp.id
  2537	    )
  2538	    select id from upd
  2539	  loop
  2540	    -- Reuse the trusted balance/status function for the FEW changed rows.
  2541	    -- total_amount just moved, so status ('paid'/'partial'/'pending') and
  2542	    -- paid_at must be re-derived. paid_amount itself is already kept in lockstep
  2543	    -- by trg_seller_payment_txns_aiud.
  2544	    perform public.refresh_seller_payment_balance(v_id);
  2545	    v_n := v_n + 1;
  2546	  end loop;
  2547	
  2548	  return v_n;
  2549	end;
  2550	$function$;
  2551	
  2552	-- refresh_open_mediabuyer_ticket
  2553	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.refresh_open_mediabuyer_ticket(p_mb uuid, p_business_id uuid, p_month date DEFAULT (date_trunc('month'::text, now()))::date)
  2554	 RETURNS void
  2555	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2556	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2557	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2558	AS $function$
  2559	declare v_month_txt text := to_char(date_trunc('month', p_month), 'YYYY-MM-01'); v_earned numeric; v_del int; v_spend numeric; v_camps int; v_id uuid; v_pt text;
  2560	begin
  2561	  if auth.uid() is not null and not public.is_owner() and not public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), array['super admin','admin','business owner']) then
  2562	    raise exception 'forbidden: admin only'; end if;
  2563	  select e.earned, e.delivered, e.spend, e.campaigns into v_earned, v_del, v_spend, v_camps from public.mb_month_earned(p_mb, p_business_id, p_month) e;
  2564	  select payment_type into v_pt from public.users where id = p_mb;
  2565	  update public.mediabuyer_payments mp
  2566	     set month_earned=v_earned, delivered_orders=v_del, total_spend=v_spend, campaigns_count=v_camps,
  2567	         commission_earned = case when v_pt='salary' then 0 else v_earned end,
  2568	         salary_earned = case when v_pt='salary' then v_earned else 0 end,
  2569	         total_amount = mp.carried_in + v_earned
  2570	   where mp.mediabuyer_id=p_mb and mp."DigiCom Business"=p_business_id and mp.payout_month=v_month_txt and mp.period_state='open'
  2571	     and (mp.month_earned is distinct from v_earned or mp.total_spend is distinct from v_spend or mp.campaigns_count is distinct from v_camps or mp.delivered_orders is distinct from v_del)
  2572	   returning mp.id into v_id;
  2573	  if v_id is not null then perform public.refresh_mediabuyer_payment_balance(v_id); end if;
  2574	end;
  2575	$function$;
  2576	
  2577	-- refresh_open_seller_ticket
  2578	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.refresh_open_seller_ticket(p_seller_id uuid, p_business_id uuid, p_month date DEFAULT (date_trunc('month'::text, now()))::date)
  2579	 RETURNS void
  2580	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2581	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2582	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2583	AS $function$
  2584	DECLARE
  2585	    v_month_txt text := to_char(date_trunc('month', p_month), 'YYYY-MM-01');
  2586	    v_earned numeric;
  2587	    v_units int;
  2588	    v_id uuid;
  2589	BEGIN
  2590	    IF auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND NOT public.is_owner() AND NOT public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner']) THEN
  2591	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden: admin only';
  2592	    END IF;
  2593	    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(commission_earned),0), COALESCE(SUM(delivered_count),0)
  2594	      INTO v_earned, v_units
  2595	      FROM public.seller_daily_earnings
  2596	     WHERE seller_id=p_seller_id AND "DigiCom Business"=p_business_id
  2597	       AND date_trunc('month', date)::date = date_trunc('month', p_month)::date;
  2598	    UPDATE public.seller_payments sp
  2599	       SET month_earned=v_earned, delivered_orders=v_units, total_amount = sp.carried_in + v_earned
  2600	     WHERE sp.seller_id=p_seller_id AND sp."DigiCom Business"=p_business_id AND sp.payout_month=v_month_txt
  2601	       AND sp.period_state='open' AND (sp.month_earned IS DISTINCT FROM v_earned OR sp.delivered_orders IS DISTINCT FROM v_units)
  2602	     RETURNING sp.id INTO v_id;
  2603	    IF v_id IS NOT NULL THEN
  2604	        PERFORM public.refresh_seller_payment_balance(v_id);
  2605	    END IF;
  2606	END;
  2607	$function$;
  2608	
  2609	-- seller_month_orders
  2610	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.seller_month_orders(p_seller uuid, p_business uuid, p_month date)
  2611	 RETURNS TABLE(order_id uuid, client text, phone text, tracking text, delivered_at timestamp with time zone, units integer, commission numeric)
  2612	 LANGUAGE sql
  2613	 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
  2614	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2615	AS $function$
  2616	    SELECT
  2617	        o.id, o.name, o.phone,
  2618	        COALESCE(
  2619	            (SELECT cp.tracking_code FROM courier_parcels cp WHERE cp.order_id = o.id AND NULLIF(btrim(cp.tracking_code), '') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY cp.created_at DESC LIMIT 1),
  2620	            (SELECT pp.tracking_code FROM company_parcels pp WHERE pp.order_id = o.id AND NULLIF(btrim(pp.tracking_code), '') IS NOT NULL ORDER BY pp.created_at DESC LIMIT 1)
  2621	        ),
  2622	        COALESCE(o.date_livraison, o.updated_at),
  2623	        CASE WHEN COALESCE(u.commission_basis, 'per_order') = 'per_unit'
  2624	             THEN sum_order_units_for_seller_products(o.id, o.seller_id)
  2625	             ELSE 1 END::integer,
  2626	        CASE WHEN COALESCE(u.commission_basis, 'per_order') = 'per_unit'
  2627	             THEN COALESCE(u.commission_price, 0) * sum_order_units_for_seller_products(o.id, o.seller_id)::numeric
  2628	             ELSE COALESCE(u.commission_price, 0) END
  2629	    FROM orders o
  2630	        JOIN users u ON u.id = o.seller_id
  2631	        JOIN package_statuses ps ON ps.id = o.package_status_id
  2632	    WHERE o.seller_id = p_seller
  2633	      AND o."DigiCom Business" IS NOT DISTINCT FROM p_business
  2634	      AND ps.is_delivered
  2635	      AND o.date_deleted IS NULL
  2636	      AND o.source_review_status IS DISTINCT FROM 'pending'
  2637	      AND COALESCE(o.swap_only, false) = false
  2638	      AND date_trunc('month', COALESCE(o.date_livraison, o.updated_at)) = date_trunc('month', p_month::timestamptz)
  2639	      AND (auth.uid() = p_seller
  2640	           OR (public.current_user_digicom_business_id() = p_business
  2641	               AND check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner'])))
  2642	    ORDER BY 5 DESC, 1;
  2643	$function$;
  2644	
  2645	-- sync_imported_orders
  2646	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_imported_orders(p_business_id uuid)
  2647	 RETURNS jsonb
  2648	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2649	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2650	 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_catalog'
  2651	AS $function$
  2652	DECLARE
  2653	  v_accessories_synced int := 0;
  2654	  v_providers_synced int := 0;
  2655	  v_rec record;
  2656	  v_best_provider_id uuid;
  2657	  v_best_provider_type text;
  2658	  v_best_fee numeric;
  2659	  v_zone_id uuid;
  2660	  v_fee_rec record;
  2661	  v_pa record;
  2662	  v_first_done boolean;
  2663	BEGIN
  2664	  -- AUTHZ: caller must operate on their OWN business. NOT owner (per-business op, owner is /owner/*-confined).
  2665	  IF NOT (public.check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin','business owner'])
  2666	          AND public.current_user_digicom_business_id() IS NOT NULL
  2667	          AND p_business_id = public.current_user_digicom_business_id()) THEN
  2668	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'forbidden: admin only' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  2669	  END IF;
  2670	
  2671	  FOR v_rec IN
  2672	    SELECT oi.id AS item_id, oi.order_id, oi.product_id, oi."DigiCom Business"
  2673	    FROM order_items oi
  2674	    WHERE oi."DigiCom Business" = p_business_id
  2675	      AND oi.product_id IS NOT NULL
  2780	      WHERE id = v_rec.id;
  2781	      v_providers_synced := v_providers_synced + 1;
  2782	    END IF;
  2783	  END LOOP;
  2784	
  2785	  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
  2786	    'accessories_synced', v_accessories_synced,
  2787	    'providers_synced', v_providers_synced
  2788	  );
  2789	END;
  2790	$function$;
  2791	
  2792	-- update_business_profile_fields
  2793	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.update_business_profile_fields(p_business_id uuid, p_name text, p_logo text, p_ice text, p_rc text, p_identifiant_fiscal text, p_patente text, p_cnss text, p_legal_form text, p_capital_social text, p_legal_address text, p_legal_city text, p_postal_code text, p_business_phone text, p_business_email text, p_website text, p_cachet_url text, p_signature_url text)
  2794	 RETURNS jsonb
  2795	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
  2796	 SECURITY DEFINER
  2797	 SET search_path TO 'public'
  2798	AS $function$
  2799	DECLARE
  2800	  v_ok boolean;
  2801	BEGIN
  2802	  -- AUTHZ: super-admin of THIS business only. NOT owner, NOT non-admin members.
  2803	  SELECT EXISTS (
  2804	    SELECT 1
  2805	    FROM public.users u
  2806	    WHERE u.id = auth.uid()
  2807	      AND u.role::text IN ('super admin','admin','business owner')
  2808	      AND u."DigiCom Business" = p_business_id
  2809	  ) INTO v_ok;
  2810	
  2811	  IF NOT v_ok THEN
  2812	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'not authorized' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  2813	  END IF;
  2814	
  2815	  IF p_name IS NULL OR length(trim(p_name)) < 2 THEN
  2816	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid name' USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  2817	  END IF;
  2818	
  2819	  UPDATE public.businesses SET
  2820	    name = trim(p_name),
  2821	    logo = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_logo, '')), ''),
  2822	    ice = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_ice, '')), ''),
  2823	    rc = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_rc, '')), ''),
  2824	    identifiant_fiscal = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_identifiant_fiscal, '')), ''),
  2825	    patente = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_patente, '')), ''),
  2826	    cnss = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_cnss, '')), ''),
  2827	    legal_form = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_legal_form, '')), ''),
  2828	    capital_social = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_capital_social, '')), ''),
  2829	    legal_address = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_legal_address, '')), ''),
  2830	    legal_city = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_legal_city, '')), ''),
  2831	    postal_code = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_postal_code, '')), ''),
  2832	    business_phone = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_business_phone, '')), ''),
  2833	    business_email = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_business_email, '')), ''),
  2834	    website = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_website, '')), ''),
  2835	    cachet_url = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_cachet_url, '')), ''),
  2836	    signature_url = NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(p_signature_url, '')), ''),
  2837	    updated_at = now()
  2838	  WHERE id = p_business_id;
  2839	
  2840	  IF NOT FOUND THEN
  2841	    RAISE EXCEPTION 'business not found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';
  2842	  END IF;
  2843	
  2844	  RETURN jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
  2845	END;

def body(sql, fn)
  start = sql.index(/create or replace function public\\.#{Regexp.escape(fn)}\\(/i)
  abort(\"missing start #{fn}\") unless start
  mark = sql.index(\"AS "'$function$", start)
  abort("missing mark #{fn}") unless mark
  from = mark + "AS $function$".length
  from += 1 if sql[from] == "'"\\n\"
  finish = sql.index(\""'$function$;", from)
  abort("missing finish #{fn}") unless finish
  sql[from...finish]
